核心发现

原始末步日志以通用触摸为主

99.97%

653,437次确认点击的紧邻前序日志为通用触摸。

同时保留原始5条与去除触摸后的5条，分别观察日志结构和具体操作。

提单页是提交前最主要的落点

38.60%

去除触摸后，最近一条行为是进入提单页。

其次为勾选协议14.38%、查看还款计划10.74%。

金额与还款计划是常见操作

26.86% / 25.71%

最近5条中明确识别到金额变动行为、查看或关闭还款计划的占比。

金额按最新五项并集；同一轨迹可同时包含多类操作。

五步轨迹集中在提交前几分钟

65.64% / 90.45%

完整5条非触摸轨迹分别集中在确认前1分钟、5分钟内。

分母为598,472次有完整5条非触摸记录的点击。

分析判断：进入提单页、金额调整、协议操作、还款计划和产品选择，构成提交前的主要可解释行为。召回引擎的前期分析应优先围绕这些行为及其发生时间展开。
01

提单前5条行为路径

按每次确认点击向前取1小时内最近5条日志；每条路径从早到晚排列，随后点击确认提单。重复日志保留，固定金额与滑动金额的提单页面统一显示为“提单页”。

序号前5条行为（从早到晚）点击次数占全部点击
当前高频路径中没有匹配结果。

展示全期高频前30条完整路径；不足5条或存在异类行为同刻并列的轨迹保留在总体统计中，不编造其完整顺序。连续两条“启动App”表示两条源日志。

02

关键操作与所在位置

以下统一观察去除通用触摸后的最近5条。最后一条行为反映临近提交的操作；五条内的行为并集反映该轨迹涉及的具体事项。

紧邻确认提单的最后一条行为

每次点击只对应一个最后位置；同刻异类行为单列顺序未知。

最近5条中明确出现过的操作

按轨迹内并集计数，不同类别可以重叠。同刻并列中无法识别的具体操作不补算。

五个位置分别以哪些行为为主

距离确认点击的位置最常见行为第二常见行为第三常见行为
重复确认：39,154次点击的最近5条里已出现过确认提单，占5.99%。可将重复确认次数、两次确认间隔列为流程分析字段，结合订单结果再区分正常重试与提交阻碍。

金额变动行为采用输入editing、滑动金额条、固定金额选项、打开金额修改、确认金额修改五项并集。本次从既有轨迹重新汇总为175,604次；此前175,652次为包含开始/结束编辑、清空的较宽分类，原冻结结果保留。借款协议操作包含查看、勾选等动作，不等于最终同意状态；红包未出现在最后5条，不代表没有使用红包。

03

五步行为距离确认点击有多久

在598,472次有完整5条非触摸记录的点击中，计算最早一条记录至本次确认点击的时差。该指标描述最后5条的时间跨度。

65.64%5条记录均在确认前1分钟内
90.45%5条记录均在确认前5分钟内
五条记录中最早一条距确认点击点击次数占完整5条样本
特征方向：除了“是否发生”，还应记录“距今多久”和“最近几次”。金额、协议、还款计划和重复确认可按1分钟、5分钟、1小时窗口提取，比较临近行为和较早行为的提单关联。
04

月度样本与轨迹覆盖

月份按确认点击的业务日期归属。11月仅覆盖1—15日；用户数和关联订单数均在各月内去重，全期总数另行去重。

各月关联续贷的确认点击次数

月份确认点击次数涉及用户数关联订单数完整5条非触摸记录完整5条覆盖率

轨迹覆盖率由1—3月约99.4%—99.5%降至5—10月约88%—89%。跨月比较路径时，需一并查看日志覆盖与App版本；覆盖率变化不能单独解释为借款意愿变化。月度用户数和订单数不能相加作为全期去重总数。

05

统计口径与召回分析应用

项目本报告口径
直接数据源wangchuanliang.user_behavior_track_record 事件明细；wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy 实际提单；tracking_points 释义用于行为名称映射。
时间范围确认点击为2026-01-01 00:00:00至2026-11-16 00:00:00，右端不含。实际事件时间取已转换为Belize时间的event_time。
确认提单按钮event_type='element_click'，控件基础码为hfmgyiwihoavrqzv或udopwuxjursbmvcm。基础码取首个下划线前缀。
续贷识别与订单关联按同一用户，在确认点击起24小时内（含同刻）找首笔全类型实际提单，再判断loan_type='reloan'。不预筛续贷、不要求审批通过或成功放款。明细无serial_id，该关联使用用户与时间。
统计单位每次确认点击为一个观察事件。相同用户、相同毫秒的多条确认日志通过权重全部保留，不按用户日去重。
前序窗口[T-1小时,T)，按实际事件时间向前取最近5条。允许跨页面；只有00:00—01:00的点击可能跨日期。不足5条不向窗口外补齐。
两种轨迹原始版取全部日志的最近5条；补充版先排除touch_change，再在同一窗口取最近5条。其余重复记录均保留。
同刻与并列第5条边界若有同刻记录，保留整组用于判定；同刻不同中文行为标签记为“同刻并列（顺序未知）”，不任意排列。完整路径榜仅展示可排序的5条。
占比分母路径、最后位置、行为并集占比均除以全部653,664次点击。五步跨度分布除以598,472次完整5条非触摸样本。合计先加计数再相除。
协议和金额开关1为勾选、0为取消；查看协议不作为同意。金额主分类按已确认的五项并集，是否发生金额净变化另行判断。
排除与核对仅user_id>0，排除黑名单5101518。只读取数；11个月汇总守恒、路径和位置分布等904项缓存核对通过。
候选信号

提取具体操作与时间

提单页访问、金额变动、还款计划、协议、产品选择及重复确认，分别提取次数、最近发生时间和短窗组合。

对照样本

比较同一时点未提单用户

使用同样的历史观察窗，比较未来3天、7天的提单率，判断哪些行为具有区分度。

策略落地

再结合风险和优惠增量

将行为意愿与风险水平组合，评估不同优惠下的新增提单与盈利，形成召回策略。

本报告还原的是已关联续贷提单的点击轨迹。行为占比不作为所有点击用户的提单转化率，也不直接证明优惠或某类操作带来提单提升。

06

附录：完整统计与汇总代码

包括完整订单关联、两种轨迹取数、中文行为编码、分月执行、全期去重审计和报告后处理。所有代码全文放在本节，可展开查看、复制或下载。

使用方式

SQL按月执行，Python脚本循环覆盖2026年1月1日—11月15日。连接使用标准PG环境变量或本机PG服务配置，代码中不保存数据库地址、账号或密码。已完成本地逻辑等价和缓存结果核对；附录整理过程未重新执行数据库查询。

# 续贷确认提单前5行为：完整复现代码

统计期为2026年1月1日至11月15日（America/Belize）。行为和订单表均只读，不建中间表，不执行业务数据修改。

## 文件

- `01_完整只读统计.sql`：完整PGSQL，可直接复制到客户端执行。默认统计1月；改顶部params的起止时间可统计其他月。包含全部中文行为映射、原始/剔除触摸两种mode、全部路径、5个位置分布、耗时分布、质量统计，无Top截断。
- `02_全期用户订单去重核对.sql`：完整期间点击、用户、订单及关联质量核对；月度去重用户数和订单数不能相加代替全期去重数。
- `run_readonly.py`：按月执行01并缓存结果，随后执行02。使用标准PG环境变量连接，无本机目录和凭据依赖。
- `summarize.py`：汇总11个月结果，核对总量，输出`statistics_result.json`。该文件的高频路径展示前30项，全部路径仍完整保存在各月`compact_YYYYMM.json`内。
- `report_facts.py`：从月度完整路径重新汇总报告事实，生成`content_facts.json`；金额行为使用最新五项并集（editing、滑动、固定选项、打开修改、确认修改），不修改历史宽口径结果；耗时仅在完整5条非触摸样本中计算。`summarize.py`会自动调用。
- `tracking_points_snapshot.json`：本次解析所用埋点字典快照（517行）。SQL已内置最终映射，运行时不需扫描tracking_points，也不受字典后续改名影响。
- `label_codes.json`：原计算程序使用的597个行为编号及中文映射；01已将编号替换为中文，不需要额外解码。

## 运行

使用Python 3.10+。安装依赖后，在本机配置`PGHOST`、`PGPORT`、`PGDATABASE`、`PGUSER`、`PGPASSWORD`、`PGSSLMODE`等标准PG连接环境变量，或使用受保护的`.pgpass`。不要把密码写入本代码包。

```sh
python3 -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python3 run_readonly.py --dws
python3 summarize.py
```

普通PostgreSQL去掉`--dws`；该选项仅在DWS中设置`query_dop=1`。默认结果写入同目录`results/`，每月成功结果原子保存，中断后重跑会复用已完成月份。需要重算现有月份时加`--force`。可通过`--out`指定取数目录，再用`summarize.py --data-dir 目录`对应汇总。

两种SQL均为只读SELECT；Python额外设置只读REPEATABLE READ事务、Belize时区、64MB work_mem及10分钟语句超时。各月份独立事务，不声明全期共用数据库快照。复现时源表若已变化，结果也会变化。

## 固定统计口径

1. 确认点击为`event_type='element_click'`，控件基础码`hfmgyiwihoavrqzv`/`udopwuxjursbmvcm`。每次点击均统计；同一用户同毫秒日志用click_weight保留次数。
2. 通过user_id匹配点击后`[T,T+24小时]`内首笔全类型实际提单，再判断`loan_type='reloan'`；不预筛续贷再找首笔，不要求放款、审批或当前订单状态。埋点无serial_id，属于时间关联。
3. 前序为同一用户`[T−1小时,T)`的最近5条；不足5条不补齐。允许跨页面，凌晨1点前可自然跨日，其他时点滚动1小时不会跨日。
4. mode=0保留原始日志；mode=1先排除touch_change，再取最近5条，其他重复不合并。p1最近、p5最早，展示按p5→p4→p3→p2→p1→确认提单。
5. 同刻不同中文行为记为“同刻并列（顺序未知）”，不强行排序；第5条的同刻边界保留整组判定。完整有序5步路径排除不足5条或并列无法排序的记录，份额仍以全部点击数为分母。
6. 通用触摸无具体控件信息；“启动App”连续日志不直接等同多次独立启动；“勾选协议”按已确认element_value=1解释，0为取消。
7. 黑名单用户5101518排除。event_time已是Belize业务时间，不再转换。页面、控件按本次冻结映射解析。

## 结果行

`grouping_code`：3完整路径；63/95/111/119/123分别为倒数第1/2/3/4/5条；125前序跨度；126最近行为距离；127总体。每种分组内`clicks`合计均等于对应mode总体。

`clicks`为加权点击数，`users`为本次SQL范围的去重用户数，`distinct_orders`为去重关联订单数；三者不可混用。`complete5`为可找到5条的点击数，`ordered_complete5`为其中无同刻不同类别并列的点击数；`ambiguous_clicks`和`boundary_clicks`用于质量核验。`span_seconds`汇总为最早选中行为至点击的秒差，`nearest_seconds`用于最近行为距离分档。

`02`按全期直接去重；不要将月度、路径或位置分组中的去重用户/订单数相加。高频路径和行为出现率属于已关联续贷提单队列的描述，不直接代表召回增量或因果效果。
01_完整只读统计.sql · 653行 · 全文
下载源文件
/* 续贷确认提单前1小时最近5条行为：完整只读统计SQL
   直接复制执行。默认统计2026年1月，修改params两处时间可切换月份；
   完整报告区间为[2026-01-01 00:00:00,2026-11-16 00:00:00)。
   建议逐月执行，避免一次扫描全期；不创建、更新或删除任何源表。
   event_time已是America/Belize时间，不重复转换。
   每次确认点击均保留，用户+毫秒合并仅用于click_weight加权，非删除重复。
   先匹配点击后24小时内首笔全产品实际提单，再要求loan_type='reloan'。
   不要求审批、获额通过、成功放款、在途或无逾期。
   前序窗口[T-1小时,T)，仅凌晨1点前可自然跨日；不跨窗补足5条。
   同刻不同行为记为并列，不任意排列，第5条同刻边界保留完整时间组。
   mode=0：原始最近5条；mode=1：排除touch_change后最近5条，其他重复仍保留。
   中文映射冻结自本次tracking_points字典和已确认控件规则。
*/
WITH params AS (
 SELECT TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01 00:00:00' AS start_time,
        TIMESTAMP '2026-02-01 00:00:00' AS end_time
), clicks AS (
 SELECT user_id,event_time AS click_time,COUNT(*)::bigint AS click_weight
 FROM wangchuanliang.user_behavior_track_record
 WHERE event_time>=(SELECT start_time FROM params) AND event_time<(SELECT end_time FROM params)
   AND event_date>=(SELECT start_time::date FROM params) AND event_date<(SELECT end_time::date FROM params)
   AND user_id>0 AND user_id<>5101518 AND event_type='element_click'
   AND (element_id LIKE 'hfmgyiwihoavrqzv%' OR element_id LIKE 'udopwuxjursbmvcm%')
   AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1) IN ('hfmgyiwihoavrqzv','udopwuxjursbmvcm')
 GROUP BY user_id,event_time
), orders AS (
 SELECT user_id,serial_id,apply_time,loan_type
 FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy
 WHERE apply_time>=(SELECT start_time FROM params) AND apply_time<(SELECT end_time + INTERVAL '24 hours' FROM params)
   AND user_id>0 AND user_id<>5101518
), next_apply AS (
 SELECT c.user_id,c.click_time,c.click_weight,MIN(o.apply_time) AS next_apply_time
 FROM clicks c LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.user_id=c.user_id
  AND o.apply_time>=c.click_time AND o.apply_time<=c.click_time+INTERVAL '24 hours'
 GROUP BY c.user_id,c.click_time,c.click_weight
), associations AS (
 SELECT n.user_id,n.click_time,n.click_weight,n.next_apply_time,
        COUNT(o.apply_time) AS next_orders,COUNT(DISTINCT o.loan_type) AS type_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.loan_type IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS unknown_types,
        COUNT(DISTINCT o.serial_id) AS next_distinct_serials,
        SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.serial_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_serials,
        MIN(o.loan_type) AS next_loan_type,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT o.serial_id)=1
                   AND SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.serial_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)=0
             THEN MIN(o.serial_id::text) END AS next_serial_id
 FROM next_apply n LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.user_id=n.user_id AND o.apply_time=n.next_apply_time
 GROUP BY n.user_id,n.click_time,n.click_weight,n.next_apply_time
), classified AS (
 SELECT a.*,CASE WHEN next_apply_time IS NULL THEN 'no_application_24h'
                 WHEN type_count<>1 OR unknown_types>0 THEN 'next_order_type_ambiguous'
                 WHEN next_loan_type='reloan' THEN 'reloan'
                 WHEN next_loan_type='firstloan' THEN 'firstloan'
                 ELSE 'other_order_type' END AS match_class
 FROM associations a
),
hours AS (
 SELECT a.*,DATE_TRUNC('hour',a.click_time)-h.n*INTERVAL '1 hour' AS event_hour
 FROM classified a CROSS JOIN (SELECT 0 AS n UNION ALL SELECT 1 AS n) h
 WHERE a.match_class='reloan'
), ranked AS (
 SELECT a.user_id,a.click_time,a.click_weight,a.next_apply_time,a.next_serial_id,
 e.event_time,e.event_type,e.page_id,e.page_type,e.element_id,e.element_type,e.element_value,
 RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY a.user_id,a.click_time ORDER BY e.event_time DESC NULLS LAST) AS raw_rank,
 RANK() OVER(PARTITION BY a.user_id,a.click_time,CASE WHEN e.event_type='touch_change' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END
             ORDER BY e.event_time DESC NULLS LAST) AS business_rank
 FROM hours a LEFT JOIN wangchuanliang.user_behavior_track_record e
 ON e.user_id=a.user_id AND DATE_TRUNC('hour',e.event_time)=a.event_hour
 AND e.event_date>=(SELECT (start_time - INTERVAL '1 hour')::date FROM params) AND e.event_date<(SELECT end_time::date FROM params)
 AND e.event_time>=(SELECT start_time - INTERVAL '1 hour' FROM params) AND e.event_time<(SELECT end_time FROM params)
 AND e.event_time>=a.click_time-INTERVAL '1 hour' AND e.event_time<a.click_time
), labelled AS (
 SELECT r.*,CASE WHEN event_type='app_start' THEN '启动App'
WHEN event_type='touch_change' THEN '通用触摸日志'
WHEN event_type='page_view' THEN CASE LEFT(page_id,16) WHEN 'xyrsaqxpwmedzlsm' THEN CASE page_type WHEN 'page_view_in' THEN '进入提单页（滑动金额）' WHEN 'page_view_out' THEN '离开提单页（滑动金额）' ELSE WHEN event_type='text_change' AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1)='evjowpwpyrixznfx' THEN '输入：手机号输入'
WHEN event_type='switch_change' AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1)='xeuwgoqcuhxauakv' THEN '是否同意注册协议'
WHEN event_type='element_click' AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1)='uozjftlwtchkgmua' THEN '协议弹窗同意按钮'
WHEN event_type='element_click' AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1)='ehpwlciojmsicuxj' THEN '协议弹窗点击查看隐私协议' ELSE '未识别行为' END AS behavior FROM ranked r
), selected AS (
 SELECT r.*,m.mode,
 CASE WHEN m.mode=1 AND event_type='touch_change' THEN NULL ELSE event_time END AS selected_time,
 CASE WHEN m.mode=1 AND event_type='touch_change' THEN -1
      WHEN m.mode=0 THEN raw_rank ELSE business_rank END AS group_rank
 FROM labelled r CROSS JOIN (SELECT 0 AS mode UNION ALL SELECT 1 AS mode) m
 WHERE event_time IS NULL
 OR (m.mode=0 AND raw_rank<=5)
 OR (m.mode=1 AND ((event_type<>'touch_change' AND business_rank<=5) OR (event_type='touch_change' AND raw_rank=1)))
), time_groups AS (
 SELECT user_id,click_time,click_weight,next_apply_time,next_serial_id,mode,selected_time,group_rank,
 COUNT(selected_time) AS n,COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN selected_time IS NOT NULL THEN behavior END) AS label_n,
 CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN selected_time IS NOT NULL THEN behavior END)>1
      THEN '同刻并列（顺序未知）' ELSE MIN(CASE WHEN selected_time IS NOT NULL THEN behavior END) END AS group_label
 FROM selected
 GROUP BY user_id,click_time,click_weight,next_apply_time,next_serial_id,mode,selected_time,group_rank
), paths AS (
 SELECT user_id,click_time,click_weight,next_apply_time,next_serial_id,mode,
 LEAST(SUM(n),5) AS prior_n,
 MAX(CASE WHEN label_n>1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS ambiguous,
 MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank+n>6 AND n>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS boundary,
 MAX(CASE WHEN selected_time::date<click_time::date THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS cross_day,
 EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM click_time-MIN(selected_time)) AS span_seconds,
 EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM click_time-MAX(selected_time)) AS nearest_seconds,
 MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank<=1 AND group_rank+n>1 THEN group_label END) AS p1,
MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank<=2 AND group_rank+n>2 THEN group_label END) AS p2,
MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank<=3 AND group_rank+n>3 THEN group_label END) AS p3,
MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank<=4 AND group_rank+n>4 THEN group_label END) AS p4,
MAX(CASE WHEN group_rank<=5 AND group_rank+n>5 THEN group_label END) AS p5
 FROM time_groups GROUP BY user_id,click_time,click_weight,next_apply_time,next_serial_id,mode
), trace_bands AS (
 SELECT p.*,
 CASE WHEN span_seconds IS NULL THEN '0_无记录' WHEN span_seconds<=10 THEN '1_10秒内'
      WHEN span_seconds<=30 THEN '2_10至30秒' WHEN span_seconds<=60 THEN '3_30至60秒'
      WHEN span_seconds<=300 THEN '4_1至5分钟' ELSE '5_5至60分钟' END AS span_bucket,
 CASE WHEN nearest_seconds IS NULL THEN '0_无记录' WHEN nearest_seconds<=1 THEN '1_1秒内'
      WHEN nearest_seconds<=5 THEN '2_1至5秒' WHEN nearest_seconds<=30 THEN '3_5至30秒'
      WHEN nearest_seconds<=60 THEN '4_30至60秒' ELSE '5_1至60分钟' END AS nearest_bucket
 FROM paths p
), aggregates AS (
 SELECT mode,p1,p2,p3,p4,p5,span_bucket,nearest_bucket,
 GROUPING(p1)*64+GROUPING(p2)*32+GROUPING(p3)*16+GROUPING(p4)*8+GROUPING(p5)*4
  +GROUPING(span_bucket)*2+GROUPING(nearest_bucket) AS grouping_code,
 SUM(click_weight)::bigint AS clicks,COUNT(DISTINCT user_id)::bigint AS users,
 SUM(CASE WHEN prior_n=5 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS complete5,
 SUM(CASE WHEN prior_n=5 AND ambiguous=0 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS ordered_complete5,
 SUM(CASE WHEN ambiguous=1 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS ambiguous_clicks,
 SUM(CASE WHEN boundary=1 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS boundary_clicks,
 SUM(CASE WHEN cross_day=1 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS cross_day_clicks,
 SUM(CASE WHEN next_apply_time-click_time<=INTERVAL '5 minutes' THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS order_5m,
 SUM(CASE WHEN next_apply_time-click_time<=INTERVAL '1 hour' THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS order_1h,
 SUM(CASE WHEN span_seconds IS NOT NULL THEN span_seconds*click_weight ELSE 0 END) AS sum_span_seconds,
 SUM(CASE WHEN span_seconds IS NOT NULL THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS valid_span_clicks,
 COUNT(DISTINCT next_serial_id)::bigint AS distinct_orders,
 SUM(CASE WHEN next_serial_id IS NULL THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END)::bigint AS nonunique_order_clicks
 FROM trace_bands GROUP BY GROUPING SETS
 ((mode,p1,p2,p3,p4,p5),(mode,p1),(mode,p2),(mode,p3),(mode,p4),(mode,p5),
  (mode,span_bucket),(mode,nearest_bucket),(mode))
)
/* grouping_code: 3=完整路径，63/95/111/119/123=倒数第1/2/3/4/5条，
   125=前序跨度分布，126=最近行为距离，127=总体。p1最近，p5最早。
   路径展示顺序 p5 → p4 → p3 → p2 → p1 → 确认提单。
   所有路径均返回；SQL不按Top N截断。 */
SELECT * FROM aggregates;
02_全期用户订单去重核对.sql · 58行 · 全文

/* Read only. Every submit-click log is retained through click_weight.
Actual event_time is already Belize timestamp; no additional time conversion.
The behavior table has no serial_id. Temporal association follows the existing
24-hour click-to-first-application convention: first ALL-type application, then
classify reloan. No approval, funding, current-status or credit-pass filters.
Prior window [T-1h,T); cross-day possible only before 01:00 by construction.
RANK retains every event at the fifth-position timestamp boundary; equal-time
events are explicitly resolved as unordered groups in analysis, never arbitrarily
ordered. Repeated events are not removed. Source tables are not modified. */
WITH clicks AS (
 SELECT user_id,event_time AS click_time,COUNT(*)::bigint AS click_weight
 FROM wangchuanliang.user_behavior_track_record
 WHERE event_time>=TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01 00:00:00' AND event_time<TIMESTAMP '2026-11-16 00:00:00'
   AND event_date>=DATE '2026-01-01' AND event_date<DATE '2026-11-16'
   AND user_id>0 AND user_id<>5101518 AND event_type='element_click'
   AND (element_id LIKE 'hfmgyiwihoavrqzv%' OR element_id LIKE 'udopwuxjursbmvcm%')
   AND SPLIT_PART(element_id,'_',1) IN ('hfmgyiwihoavrqzv','udopwuxjursbmvcm')
 GROUP BY user_id,event_time
), orders AS (
 SELECT user_id,serial_id,apply_time,loan_type
 FROM wangchuanliang.order_loan_f_v2_copy
 WHERE apply_time>=TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01 00:00:00' AND apply_time<TIMESTAMP '2026-11-17 00:00:00'
   AND user_id>0 AND user_id<>5101518
), next_apply AS (
 SELECT c.user_id,c.click_time,c.click_weight,MIN(o.apply_time) AS next_apply_time
 FROM clicks c LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.user_id=c.user_id
  AND o.apply_time>=c.click_time AND o.apply_time<=c.click_time+INTERVAL '24 hours'
 GROUP BY c.user_id,c.click_time,c.click_weight
), associations AS (
 SELECT n.user_id,n.click_time,n.click_weight,n.next_apply_time,
        COUNT(o.apply_time) AS next_orders,COUNT(DISTINCT o.loan_type) AS type_count,
        SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.loan_type IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS unknown_types,
        COUNT(DISTINCT o.serial_id) AS next_distinct_serials,
        SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.serial_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS missing_serials,
        MIN(o.loan_type) AS next_loan_type,
        CASE WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT o.serial_id)=1
                   AND SUM(CASE WHEN o.apply_time IS NOT NULL AND o.serial_id IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)=0
             THEN MIN(o.serial_id::text) END AS next_serial_id
 FROM next_apply n LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.user_id=n.user_id AND o.apply_time=n.next_apply_time
 GROUP BY n.user_id,n.click_time,n.click_weight,n.next_apply_time
), classified AS (
 SELECT a.*,CASE WHEN next_apply_time IS NULL THEN 'no_application_24h'
                 WHEN type_count<>1 OR unknown_types>0 THEN 'next_order_type_ambiguous'
                 WHEN next_loan_type='reloan' THEN 'reloan'
                 WHEN next_loan_type='firstloan' THEN 'firstloan'
                 ELSE 'other_order_type' END AS match_class
 FROM associations a
)
SELECT DATE_TRUNC('month',click_time)::date AS click_month,
GROUPING(DATE_TRUNC('month',click_time)::date) AS is_total,match_class,
COUNT(*) AS anchor_groups,SUM(click_weight) AS click_logs,COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) AS distinct_users,
COUNT(DISTINCT next_serial_id) AS distinct_orders,
SUM(CASE WHEN next_orders>1 THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END) AS tied_order_clicks,
SUM(CASE WHEN next_serial_id IS NULL AND next_apply_time IS NOT NULL THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END) AS nonunique_order_clicks,
MIN(click_time) AS first_click,MAX(click_time) AS last_click,
SUM(CASE WHEN next_apply_time-click_time<=INTERVAL '5 minutes' THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END) AS order_5m,
SUM(CASE WHEN next_apply_time-click_time<=INTERVAL '1 hour' THEN click_weight ELSE 0 END) AS order_1h
FROM classified GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((DATE_TRUNC('month',click_time)::date,match_class),(match_class));
run_readonly.py · 139行 · 全文

"""只读分月复现：PG连接参数来自标准PG环境变量，无业务表写入。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

import psycopg2

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
START_MARKER = "TIMESTAMP '2026-01-01 00:00:00' AS start_time"
END_MARKER = "TIMESTAMP '2026-02-01 00:00:00' AS end_time"
GROUPS = (3, 63, 95, 111, 119, 123, 125, 126, 127)


def json_value(value):
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return float(value)
    if isinstance(value, (date, datetime)):
        return value.isoformat()
    raise TypeError(type(value).__name__)


def save_json(path, data):
    temporary = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + '.partial')
    temporary.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2,
                                    default=json_value), encoding='utf-8')
    temporary.replace(path)


def connect(dws=False):
    # libpq自动读取PGHOST、PGPORT、PGDATABASE、PGUSER、PGPASSWORD、PGSSLMODE等。
    conn = psycopg2.connect(connect_timeout=15,
                            application_name='reloan_pre5_readonly_reproduction')
    conn.set_session(readonly=True, isolation_level='REPEATABLE READ')
    with conn.cursor() as cur:
        cur.execute("SET LOCAL TIME ZONE 'America/Belize'")
        cur.execute("SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '10min'")
        cur.execute("SET LOCAL work_mem = '64MB'")
        if dws:
            cur.execute('SET LOCAL query_dop = 1')
        cur.execute("SELECT current_setting('transaction_read_only')")
        assert cur.fetchone()[0] == 'on', '必须使用只读事务'
    return conn


def query(sql, dws=False):
    conn = connect(dws)
    began = time.monotonic()
    try:
        with conn.cursor() as cur:
            cur.execute(sql)
            columns = [column[0] for column in cur.description]
            rows = [dict(zip(columns, row)) for row in cur.fetchall()]
    finally:
        conn.rollback()
        conn.close()
    return rows, round(time.monotonic() - began, 3)


def monthly_bounds(start, end):
    current = start
    while current < end:
        following = (datetime(current.year + 1, 1, 1) if current.month == 12
                     else datetime(current.year, current.month + 1, 1))
        following = min(following, end)
        yield current, following
        current = following


def check_month(rows):
    # 9种分组分别守恒：任一路径/位置/时间桶的点击数都等于总体。
    for mode in (0, 1):
        subset = [row for row in rows if row['mode'] == mode]
        total = next(row['clicks'] for row in subset if row['grouping_code'] == 127)
        for group in GROUPS:
            count = sum(row['clicks'] for row in subset if row['grouping_code'] == group)
            assert count == total, (mode, group, count, total)
        all_paths = [row for row in subset if row['grouping_code'] == 3]
        ordered_complete = sum(row['clicks'] for row in all_paths
                               if all(row['p' + str(i)] is not None and
                                      row['p' + str(i)] != '同刻并列（顺序未知）'
                                      for i in range(1, 6)))
        expected = next(row['ordered_complete5'] for row in subset
                        if row['grouping_code'] == 127)
        assert ordered_complete == expected, (mode, ordered_complete, expected)


def period_sql(template, start, end):
    # start/end已经解析为datetime；只替换唯一的日期常量，不拼入任意SQL。
    assert template.count(START_MARKER) == template.count(END_MARKER) == 1
    return template.replace(START_MARKER,
                            "TIMESTAMP '" + str(start) + "' AS start_time").replace(
                                END_MARKER,
                                "TIMESTAMP '" + str(end) + "' AS end_time")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--out', type=Path, default=BASE / 'results')
    parser.add_argument('--dws', action='store_true',
                        help='DWS环境设置query_dop=1；普通PostgreSQL不使用此参数')
    parser.add_argument('--force', action='store_true', help='重算并替换已有成功缓存')
    parser.add_argument('--audit-only', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    template = (BASE / '01_完整只读统计.sql').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    start, end = datetime(2026, 1, 1), datetime(2026, 11, 16)
    if not args.audit_only:
        for left, right in monthly_bounds(start, end):
            name = 'compact_' + left.strftime('%Y%m')
            target = args.out / (name + '.json')
            if target.exists() and not args.force:
                print('复用已完成月份：' + name, flush=True)
                continue
            sql = period_sql(template, left, right)
            print('执行月份：' + name, flush=True)
            rows, seconds = query(sql, args.dws)
            check_month(rows)
            (args.out / (name + '.sql')).write_text(sql, encoding='utf-8')
            save_json(target, dict(start=str(left), end=str(right), read_only=True,
                                   seconds=seconds, rows=rows))
            print('完成：' + name + '，行数=' + str(len(rows)), flush=True)
    # 全期去重用户数/订单数不可把月度去重数相加，须单独全期核对。
    audit = args.out / 'global_association_audit.json'
    if args.force or not audit.exists():
        sql = (BASE / '02_全期用户订单去重核对.sql').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        print('执行全期去重核对', flush=True)
        rows, seconds = query(sql, args.dws)
        save_json(audit, dict(start=str(start), end=str(end), read_only=True,
                              seconds=seconds, rows=rows))
    print('取数完成。运行 summarize.py 汇总结果。', flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
summarize.py · 96行 · 全文
下载源文件
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
import json
import argparse

W=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'results'
POSITION={63:1,95:2,111:3,119:4,123:5}
ADDITIVE=('clicks','complete5','ordered_complete5','ambiguous_clicks','boundary_clicks',
          'cross_day_clicks','order_5m','order_1h','sum_span_seconds','valid_span_clicks','nonunique_order_clicks')


def summarize(partial=False):
    files=sorted(W.glob('compact_2026[0-9][0-9].json'))
    if not partial:assert len(files)==11,len(files)
    totals={m:Counter() for m in (0,1)};paths={m:Counter() for m in (0,1)}
    positions={m:{k:Counter() for k in range(1,6)} for m in (0,1)}
    spans={m:Counter() for m in (0,1)};nearest={m:Counter() for m in (0,1)};monthly=[]
    for file in files:
        d=json.loads(file.read_text());month=d['start'][:7]
        for mode in (0,1):
            rs=[r for r in d['rows'] if r['mode']==mode]
            summary=next(r for r in rs if r['grouping_code']==127)
            monthly.append(dict(month=month,mode=mode,**{k:v for k,v in summary.items() if k not in ('mode','p1','p2','p3','p4','p5','span_bucket','nearest_bucket','grouping_code')}))
            for k in ADDITIVE:totals[mode][k]+=summary[k]
            for g in (3,63,95,111,119,123,125,126):
                n=sum(r['clicks'] for r in rs if r['grouping_code']==g)
                assert n==summary['clicks'],(file.name,mode,g,n,summary['clicks'])
            for r in rs:
                g=r['grouping_code'];n=r['clicks']
                if g==3:paths[mode][tuple(r['p'+str(k)] for k in range(5,0,-1))]+=n
                elif g in POSITION:
                    k=POSITION[g];positions[mode][k][r['p'+str(k)] or '无前序记录']+=n
                elif g==125:spans[mode][r['span_bucket']]+=n
                elif g==126:nearest[mode][r['nearest_bucket']]+=n
    assert totals[0]['clicks']==totals[1]['clicks']
    out=dict(period='2026-01-01至2026-11-15',months=len(files),partial=partial,monthly=monthly,modes={})
    for mode in (0,1):
        total=totals[mode]['clicks'];assert sum(paths[mode].values())==total
        for k in range(1,6):assert sum(positions[mode][k].values())==total
        ordered=Counter({p:n for p,n in paths[mode].items() if '同刻并列（顺序未知）' not in p})
        full=Counter({p:n for p,n in ordered.items() if None not in p})
        assert sum(full.values())==totals[mode]['ordered_complete5']
        feature=Counter()
        for p,n in paths[mode].items():
            flags=set()
            for s in p:
                if not s:continue
                if s in ('打开修改金额','清空借款金额','确认修改金额','选择固定借款金额','滑动调整借款金额','开始编辑借款金额','编辑借款金额','结束编辑借款金额'):flags.add('金额操作')
                if s in ('打开借款期限选择','关闭借款期限选择','选择分期或产品'):flags.add('账期/产品选择')
                if '息费券' in s:flags.add('息费券操作')
                if '提额券' in s:flags.add('提额券操作')
                if '协议' in s:flags.add('借款协议操作')
                if s in ('查看还款计划','关闭还款计划'):flags.add('查看/关闭还款计划')
                if s=='点击确认提单':flags.add('前序再次点击确认提单')
            for f in flags:feature[f]+=n
        def render(c,limit):return [dict(path=[s for s in p if s is not None],steps=sum(s is not None for s in p),clicks=n,share=n/total) for p,n in c.most_common(limit)]
        def normalize(s):
            if s is None:return None
            return s.replace('提单页（固定金额）','提单页').replace('提单页（滑动金额）','提单页').replace('首页大按钮','点击首页主按钮')
        normalized=Counter()
        for p,n in full.items():normalized[tuple(normalize(s) for s in p)]+=n
        normalized_positions=[]
        for k in range(1,6):
            counts=Counter()
            for s,n in positions[mode][k].items():counts[normalize(s)]+=n
            normalized_positions.append(dict(position=k,rows=[dict(behavior=s,clicks=n,share=n/total) for s,n in counts.most_common(20)]))
        out['modes'][str(mode)]=dict(summary=dict(totals[mode]),top_full_paths=render(full,30),
           top_ordered_paths_any_length=render(ordered,30),
           top_full_paths_pages_merged=render(normalized,30),positions_pages_merged=normalized_positions,
           positions=[dict(position=k,rows=[dict(behavior=s,clicks=n,share=n/total) for s,n in positions[mode][k].most_common(20)]) for k in range(1,6)],
           span_bands=[dict(band=b,clicks=n,share=n/total) for b,n in sorted(spans[mode].items())],
           nearest_bands=[dict(band=b,clicks=n,share=n/total) for b,n in sorted(nearest[mode].items())],
           feature_presence_definite=[dict(feature=f,clicks=n,share=n/total) for f,n in feature.most_common()],
           distinct_path_patterns=len(paths[mode]),checks_passed=True)
    audit_path=W/'global_association_audit.json'
    if audit_path.exists():
        audit=json.loads(audit_path.read_text())
        g=next(r for r in audit['rows'] if r['is_total']==1 and r['match_class']=='reloan')
        if not partial:assert out['modes']['0']['summary']['clicks']==int(g['click_logs'])
        out['global_association']=g
        for m in monthly:
            r=next(a for a in audit['rows'] if a['is_total']==0 and a['match_class']=='reloan' and a['click_month'].startswith(m['month']))
            assert m['clicks']==int(r['click_logs'])
    (W/('partial_summary.json' if partial else 'statistics_result.json')).write_text(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    for m,v in out['modes'].items():
        print('MODE',m,'SUMMARY',v['summary'])
        print('TOP FULL',json.dumps(v['top_full_paths'][:10],ensure_ascii=False))
        print('POSITIONS',json.dumps([dict(position=x['position'],rows=x['rows'][:5]) for x in v['positions']],ensure_ascii=False))
    print('COMPLETED MONTHS',len(files),flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--partial',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--data-dir',type=Path,default=W);a=p.parse_args();W=a.data_dir.resolve();summarize(a.partial)
    if not a.partial:
        from report_facts import generate
        generate(W)
report_facts.py · 121行 · 全文

"""仅从完整月度缓存生成报告事实；不查数据库，不改原始statistics_result.json。"""
from collections import Counter
import json
from pathlib import Path

AMOUNT_LABELS = {
    '编辑借款金额',       # element_type=editing，单独start_edit/end_edit不算
    '滑动调整借款金额',
    '选择固定借款金额',
    '打开修改金额',
    '确认修改金额',
}
HISTORICAL_AMOUNT_LABELS = AMOUNT_LABELS | {
    '清空借款金额', '开始编辑借款金额', '结束编辑借款金额',
}


def generate(data_dir):
    data_dir = Path(data_dir)
    src = json.loads((data_dir / 'statistics_result.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    files = sorted(data_dir.glob('compact_2026[0-9][0-9].json'))
    assert len(files) == 11
    total = src['modes']['0']['summary']['clicks']
    paths = {m: Counter() for m in (0, 1)}
    span_complete = {m: Counter() for m in (0, 1)}
    monthly = []
    for file in files:
        rows = json.loads(file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['rows']
        summaries = {}
        for mode in (0, 1):
            subset = [row for row in rows if row['mode'] == mode]
            summary = next(row for row in subset if row['grouping_code'] == 127)
            summaries[mode] = summary
            for group in (3, 63, 95, 111, 119, 123, 125, 126):
                assert sum(row['clicks'] for row in subset
                           if row['grouping_code'] == group) == summary['clicks']
            for row in subset:
                if row['grouping_code'] == 3:
                    path = tuple(row['p' + str(i)] for i in range(5, 0, -1))
                    paths[mode][path] += row['clicks']
                elif row['grouping_code'] == 125:
                    # 取完整5条的分子，不能用全部点击桶数替代完整5条桶数。
                    span_complete[mode][row['span_bucket']] += row['complete5']
        monthly.append({
            'month': file.stem[-6:], 'clicks': summaries[0]['clicks'],
            'users': summaries[0]['users'],
            'distinct_orders': summaries[0]['distinct_orders'],
            'complete5_non_touch': summaries[1]['complete5'],
            'complete5_non_touch_share': summaries[1]['complete5'] / summaries[1]['clicks'],
        })
    assert sum(paths[0].values()) == sum(paths[1].values()) == total
    complete_denominator = sum(span_complete[1].values())
    assert complete_denominator == src['modes']['1']['summary']['complete5']
    latest_count = historical_count = difference = 0
    single_counts, extra_labels = Counter(), Counter()
    for path, count in paths[1].items():
        labels = set(path)
        latest_count += count * bool(labels & AMOUNT_LABELS)
        historical_count += count * bool(labels & HISTORICAL_AMOUNT_LABELS)
        for label in labels & AMOUNT_LABELS:
            single_counts[label] += count
        if labels & HISTORICAL_AMOUNT_LABELS and not labels & AMOUNT_LABELS:
            difference += count
            for label in labels & HISTORICAL_AMOUNT_LABELS:
                extra_labels[label] += count
    spans = [dict(band=band, clicks=count,
                  share_complete5=count / complete_denominator,
                  share_all_clicks=count / total)
             for band, count in sorted(span_complete[1].items()) if band != '0_无记录']
    within60 = sum(count for band, count in span_complete[1].items()
                   if band in ('1_10秒内', '2_10至30秒', '3_30至60秒'))
    within300 = within60 + span_complete[1]['4_1至5分钟']
    audit = {key: int(src['global_association'][key]) for key in (
        'distinct_users', 'distinct_orders', 'anchor_groups', 'order_5m', 'order_1h')}
    facts = {
        'purpose': '从完整月度结果生成报告事实；不访问数据库，不修改历史统计结果。',
        'global': {
            'clicks': total, 'users_distinct': audit['distinct_users'],
            'orders_distinct': audit['distinct_orders'],
            'click_anchor_groups': audit['anchor_groups'],
            'order_5m': audit['order_5m'],
            'order_5m_share_conditional': audit['order_5m'] / total,
            'order_1h': audit['order_1h'],
            'order_1h_share_conditional': audit['order_1h'] / total,
        },
        'raw_nearest_touch': src['modes']['0']['positions_pages_merged'][0]['rows'][0],
        'raw_top_complete_paths': src['modes']['0']['top_full_paths_pages_merged'][:8],
        'non_touch_top_complete_paths': src['modes']['1']['top_full_paths_pages_merged'][:12],
        'non_touch_last_position': src['modes']['1']['positions_pages_merged'][0]['rows'],
        'non_touch_complete5_span': {
            'denominator': complete_denominator, 'bands': spans,
            'within60_seconds_clicks': within60,
            'within60_seconds_share': within60 / complete_denominator,
            'within300_seconds_clicks': within300,
            'within300_seconds_share': within300 / complete_denominator,
            'definition': '从最近5条非touch日志中最早一条的event_time到确认提单点击的时差；完整5条样本计算，含同刻并列。',
        },
        'amount_union_20261118': {
            'definition': '前5条非touch行为中，金额输入editing、滑动金额条、固定金额选项、打开金额修改、确认金额修改的可识别并集。',
            'labels': sorted(AMOUNT_LABELS), 'clicks': latest_count,
            'share': latest_count / total, 'single_counts': dict(single_counts),
            'historical_wide_definition_clicks': historical_count,
            'historical_wide_share': historical_count / total,
            'difference_clicks': difference, 'historical_extra_labels': dict(extra_labels),
            'note': '最新五项并集排除单独出现的start_edit/end_edit及清空金额；仅从现存路径缓存重汇总，同刻并列标签可能遮蔽具体行为，计数仅称明确识别。历史statistics_result.json保持原分类。',
        },
        'quality': {mode: {key: value for key, value in src['modes'][mode]['summary'].items()
                           if key != 'sum_span_seconds'} for mode in ('0', '1')},
        'monthly': monthly,
    }
    target = data_dir / 'content_facts.json'
    target.write_text(json.dumps(facts, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    return facts


if __name__ == '__main__':
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--data-dir', type=Path,
                        default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent / 'results')
    generate(parser.parse_args().data_dir)
